This release 44 added, 3710 changed API endpoint(s).

:::danger Breaking change
This release removes endpoints or adds newly-required request fields. Review the **Removed** and **Changed** sections before upgrading your integration.
:::

<!-- truncate -->

## Added

- `DELETE /api/vendor-credits/{vendor_credit}/payments/{payment}` — Delete Vendor Credit Refund Payment
- `GET /api/vendor-credits/for-invoice/{purchase_invoice}` — List Applicable Vendor Credits for Invoice
- `GET /api/vendor-credits/for-supplier/{supplier}` — List Open Vendor Credits for Supplier
- `GET /api/vendor-credits/shipments/{shipment}` — Get Return Shipment
- `GET /api/vendor-credits/shipments/{shipment}/activity-log` — Get Return Shipment Activity Log
- `GET /api/vendor-credits/stats` — Get Vendor Credit Stats
- `GET /api/vendor-credits/{vendorCredit}/activity-log` — Get Vendor Credit Activity Log
- `GET /api/vendor-credits/{vendor_credit}/fifo-layer-usages` — List Vendor Credit FIFO Layer Usages
- `GET /api/vendor-credits/{vendor_credit}/inventory-movements` — List Vendor Credit Inventory Movements
- `GET /api/vendor-deposits/cash-due-schedule` — Get Cash Due Schedule
- `GET /api/vendor-deposits/cash-flow-forecast` — Get Cash Flow Forecast
- `GET /api/vendor-deposits/exceptions-queue` — Get Exceptions Queue
- `GET /api/vendor-deposits/expected-milestones` — List Expected Deposit Milestones
- `GET /api/vendor-deposits/po-by-shipping-stage` — Get Purchase Orders by Shipping Stage
- `GET /api/vendor-deposits/supplier-exposure` — Get Supplier Exposure Report
- `GET /api/vendor-deposits/upcoming-liability` — Get Upcoming Liability Report
- `GET /api/vendor-deposits/write-off-report` — Get Vendor Deposit Write-Off Report
- `GET /api/vendor-deposits/{vendorDepositId}/accounting` — Get Vendor Deposit Accounting Transactions
- `GET /api/vendor-deposits/{vendorDepositId}/applicable-bills` — List Applicable Bills for Vendor Deposit
- `GET /api/vendor-deposits/{vendorDepositId}/approval-steps` — List Vendor Deposit Approval Steps
- `POST /api/vendor-credits/bulk-void` — Bulk Void Vendor Credits
- `POST /api/vendor-credits/from-invoice/{purchase_invoice}` — Create Vendor Credit from Invoice
- `POST /api/vendor-credits/paid` — Record Vendor Credit Refund Payment
- `POST /api/vendor-credits/{vendor_credit}/authorize` — Authorize Vendor Credit
- `POST /api/vendor-credits/{vendor_credit}/auto-apply` — Auto-Apply Vendor Credit
- `POST /api/vendor-credits/{vendor_credit}/reopen` — Reopen Vendor Credit
- `POST /api/vendor-credits/{vendor_credit}/unauthorize` — Unauthorize Vendor Credit
- `POST /api/vendor-deposits/bulk-apply` — Bulk Apply Vendor Deposits
- `POST /api/vendor-deposits/bulk-approve` — Bulk Approve Vendor Deposits
- `POST /api/vendor-deposits/bulk-create` — Bulk Create Vendor Deposits
- `POST /api/vendor-deposits/import` — Import Vendor Deposits
- `POST /api/vendor-deposits/import/preview` — Preview Vendor Deposit Import
- `POST /api/vendor-deposits/{vendorDepositId}/applications/preview` — Preview Vendor Deposit Application
- `POST /api/vendor-deposits/{vendorDepositId}/approval-steps/approve` — Approve Vendor Deposit Approval Step
- `POST /api/vendor-deposits/{vendorDepositId}/approval-steps/reject` — Reject Vendor Deposit Approval Step
- `POST /api/vendor-deposits/{vendorDepositId}/generate-accounting-transaction` — Generate Vendor Deposit Accounting Transaction
- `POST /api/vendor-deposits/{vendorDepositId}/push-to-qbo` — Push Vendor Deposit to QuickBooks Online
- `POST /api/vendor-deposits/{vendorDepositId}/push-to-xero` — Push Vendor Deposit to Xero
- `POST /api/vendor-deposits/{vendorDepositId}/revert-to-draft` — Revert Vendor Deposit to Draft
- `POST /api/vendor-deposits/{vendorDepositId}/void` — Void Vendor Deposit
- `PUT /api/vendor-credits/{vendorCredit}/notes/{noteId}/toggle-pin` — Toggle Vendor Credit Note Pin
- `PUT /api/vendor-credits/{vendor_credit}/payments/{payment}` — Update Vendor Credit Refund Payment
- `PUT /api/vendor-credits/{vendor_credit}/tags` — Update Vendor Credit Tags
- `PUT /api/vendor-deposits/{vendorDepositId}/purchase-order` — Link Vendor Deposit to Purchase Order
